#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

--- hash ---
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

 // Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
-void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
+void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                     const size_t numOptions,
+                     const size_t firstOption = 0) {
     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
     options.resize(numOptions);
     
     for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = firstOption + i;
         // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
-        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
+        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[globalIndex % testOptions.size()];

+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));

 }
 
+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                   : 0;
+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,6 +288,13 @@

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double sqrtT = std::sqrt(T);
    const double sigmaSqrtT = sigma * sqrtT;
    const double d1 = (std::log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / sigmaSqrtT;
    const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtT;
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = std::exp(-r * T);
    const double discountedSpot = S * std::exp(-q * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = discountedSpot * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - discountedSpot * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
                     const size_t numOptions,
                     const size_t firstOption = 0) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        const size_t globalIndex = firstOption + i;
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[globalIndex % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
                  const int rank,
                  const int worldSize,
                  size_t& firstOption,
                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);

    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
}

// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
                   const size_t firstOption,
                   const size_t totalOptions,
                   const int rank,
                   const int worldSize,
                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
    if (rank == 0) {
        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
    }

    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());

    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
        std::vector<int> displacements;
        if (rank == 0) {
            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
            displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
                size_t sourceCount = 0;
                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
            }
        }

        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
                    MPI_DOUBLE,
                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                    MPI_DOUBLE,
                    0,
                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        return;
    }

    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
    if (rank == 0) {
        if (!localResults.empty()) {
            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
        }
        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
            size_t sourceCount = 0;
            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
                         static_cast<int>(count),
                         MPI_DOUBLE,
                         source,
                         0,
                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
            }
        }
    } else {
        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
                     static_cast<int>(count),
                     MPI_DOUBLE,
                     0,
                     0,
                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }
}

// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
                             const size_t firstOption,
                             const size_t totalOptions,
                             const int rank,
                             const int worldSize,
                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
    if (rank == 0) {
        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
    }

    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
    std::vector<int> displacements;
    if (rank == 0) {
        receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
            size_t sourceCount = 0;
            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
            }
        }
    }

    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
                                   : 0;
    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
                MPI_DOUBLE,
                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                MPI_DOUBLE,
                0,
                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    int rank = 0;
    int worldSize = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);

    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }

    size_t firstOption = 0;
    size_t numLocalOptions = 0;
    getWorkRange(numOptions, rank, worldSize, firstOption, numLocalOptions);
    
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numLocalOptions, firstOption);
    
    std::vector<double> results(numLocalOptions);
    
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Pricing options...\n");
    }

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numLocalOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n",
               duration > 0.0 ? static_cast<double>(numOptions) / duration : 0.0);
    }
    
    std::vector<double> globalResults;
    if (printResults) {
        gatherResults(results, firstOption, numOptions, rank, worldSize, globalResults);
        if (rank == 0) {
            print_results(globalResults, "OptionPrices");
        }
    }
    
    int exitCode = 0;
    if (validate) {
        std::vector<double> validationResults;
        if (printResults) {
            const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);
            validationResults.assign(globalResults.begin(), globalResults.begin() + numChecks);
        } else {
            gatherValidationResults(results, firstOption, numOptions, rank, worldSize, validationResults);
        }

        if (rank == 0) {
            std::vector<OptionInput> validationOptions;
            generateOptions(validationOptions, validationResults.size());
            printf("Validating results...\n");
            const bool valid = validateResults(validationOptions, validationResults);
            if (valid) {
                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            } else {
                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
                exitCode = 1;
            }
        }
    }

    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return exitCode;
}

codex
The MPI implementation is in place. The default path performs no result transfer: it synchronizes once for timing and reduces one timing value to rank 0. I’m building it now, then I’ll compare serial and multi-rank `-r` output hashes and exercise validation, including zero/small workloads.

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                   : 0;
+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE,

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                   : 0;
+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     bool allPassed = true;

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                   : 0;
+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,6 +288,13 @@

codex
The build succeeded, and serial output is correct. Multi-rank result values/hash matched serial, but Open MPI exposed a portability bug in my `MPI_Gatherv` call: non-root ranks were passing null receive-count/displacement arrays, which some implementations still inspect. I’m fixing that by providing valid arrays on every rank, then rerunning the same tests.
apply patch

+                             const size_t firstOption,

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    receiveCounts.data(),
+                    displacements.data(),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,

+                             const int worldSize,

+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);

+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+

+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)

+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    receiveCounts.data(),
+                    displacements.data(),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                   : 0;
+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
+                receiveCounts.data(),
+                displacements.data(),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

 bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,6 +284,13 @@

     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
     options.resize(numOptions);
     
     for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = firstOption + i;
         // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
-        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
+        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[globalIndex % testOptions.size()];
         options[i] = base;
         
         // Add some variation for larger datasets
-        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
         options[i].spot *= factor;
         options[i].strike *= factor;
     }
 }
 
+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  MPI_Gatherv is used for the
+// normal case.  The chunked fallback keeps the interface usable for option
+// counts larger than the legacy int count/displacement limits.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    const bool fitsLegacyMpiCounts =
+        totalOptions <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (fitsLegacyMpiCounts) {
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+                size_t sourceCount = 0;
+                getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceCount);
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.empty() ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                    static_cast<int>(localResults.size()),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 && !globalResults.empty() ? globalResults.data() : nullptr,
+                    receiveCounts.data(),
+                    displacements.data(),
+                    MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+    std::vector<int> displacements(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize), 0);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst));
+                displacements[static_cast<size_t>(source)] = static_cast<int>(sourceFirst);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                   ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                   : 0;
+    MPI_Gatherv(localChecks == 0 ? nullptr : localResults.data(),
+                static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 && !validationResults.empty() ? validationResults.data() : nullptr,
+                receiveCounts.data(),
+                displacements.data(),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
 bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,6 +284,13 @@

 // Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
-void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
+void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                     const size_t numOptions,
+                     const size_t firstOption = 0) {
     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
     options.resize(numOptions);
     
     for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const size_t globalIndex = firstOption + i;
         // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
-        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
+        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[globalIndex % testOptions.size()];

+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (globalIndex / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    constexpr int validationTag = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                       ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                       : 0;
+        if (localChecks != 0) {
+            std::copy_n(localResults.begin(), localChecks, validationResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst);
+                MPI_Recv(validationResults.data() + sourceFirst,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         validationTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (firstOption < numChecks) {
+        const size_t localChecks = std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption);
+        MPI_Send(localResults.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,
+                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}
+

                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    constexpr int validationTag = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                       ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                       : 0;
+        if (localChecks != 0) {
+            std::copy_n(localResults.begin(), localChecks, validationResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst);
+                MPI_Recv(validationResults.data() + sourceFirst,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         validationTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (firstOption < numChecks) {
+        const size_t localChecks = std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption);
+        MPI_Send(localResults.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,
+                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}
