+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    constexpr int validationTag = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                       ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                       : 0;
+        if (localChecks != 0) {
+            std::copy_n(localResults.begin(), localChecks, validationResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst);
+                MPI_Recv(validationResults.data() + sourceFirst,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         validationTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (firstOption < numChecks) {
+        const size_t localChecks = std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption);
+        MPI_Send(localResults.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,
+                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}
+

     bool allPassed = true;

codex
The remaining crash is now isolated: with `-r -v`, non-root ranks were trying to slice rank 0’s intentionally empty `globalResults` vector after the full gather. I’m restricting that slice to rank 0; the `-v`-only path already runs cleanly across ranks.

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    constexpr int validationTag = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                       ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                       : 0;
+        if (localChecks != 0) {
+            std::copy_n(localResults.begin(), localChecks, validationResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst);
+                MPI_Recv(validationResults.data() + sourceFirst,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         validationTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (firstOption < numChecks) {
+        const size_t localChecks = std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption);
+        MPI_Send(localResults.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,
+                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}
+
 bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,6 +261,13 @@

+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,

+                         MPI_DOUBLE,

+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Gather only the prefix needed by validation.  This keeps validation cheap
+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const size_t firstOption,
+                             const size_t totalOptions,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int worldSize,
+                             std::vector<double>& validationResults) {
+    const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), totalOptions);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        validationResults.resize(numChecks);
+    }
+
+    constexpr int validationTag = 1;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const size_t localChecks = firstOption < numChecks
+                                       ? std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption)
+                                       : 0;
+        if (localChecks != 0) {
+            std::copy_n(localResults.begin(), localChecks, validationResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst);
+                MPI_Recv(validationResults.data() + sourceFirst,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         validationTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (firstOption < numChecks) {
+        const size_t localChecks = std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption);
+        MPI_Send(localResults.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,
+                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}

+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.

+                                       : 0;
+        if (localChecks != 0) {
+            std::copy_n(localResults.begin(), localChecks, validationResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+

+            if (sourceFirst < numChecks) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(sourceCount, numChecks - sourceFirst);
+                MPI_Recv(validationResults.data() + sourceFirst,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         validationTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (firstOption < numChecks) {
+        const size_t localChecks = std::min(localResults.size(), numChecks - firstOption);
+        MPI_Send(localResults.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localChecks),
+                 MPI_DOUBLE,
+                 0,
+                 validationTag,
+                 MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }

+// Split the global option array into balanced contiguous ranges.  Regenerating
+// each range locally avoids broadcasting the input data to every rank.
+void getWorkRange(const size_t totalOptions,
+                  const int rank,
+                  const int worldSize,
+                  size_t& firstOption,
+                  size_t& numLocalOptions) noexcept {
+    const size_t workers = static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t base = totalOptions / workers;
+    const size_t extra = totalOptions % workers;
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+
+    numLocalOptions = base + (rankIndex < extra ? 1 : 0);
+    firstOption = base * rankIndex + std::min(rankIndex, extra);
+}
+
+// Gather full results in global order for -r.  This path is intentionally
+// separate from the timed computation and uses bounded messages so it also
+// works with MPI implementations exposing only the legacy int count API.
+void gatherResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                   const size_t firstOption,
+                   const size_t totalOptions,
+                   const int rank,
+                   const int worldSize,
+                   std::vector<double>& globalResults) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalResults.resize(totalOptions);
+    }
+
+    constexpr size_t chunkSize = 1U << 20;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (!localResults.empty()) {
+            std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(), globalResults.begin() + firstOption);
+        }
+        for (int source = 1; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+            size_t sourceFirst = 0;
+            size_t sourceCount = 0;
+            getWorkRange(totalOptions, source, worldSize, sourceFirst, sourceCount);
+            for (size_t offset = 0; offset < sourceCount; offset += chunkSize) {
+                const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, sourceCount - offset);
+                MPI_Recv(globalResults.data() + sourceFirst + offset,
+                         static_cast<int>(count),
+                         MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source,
+                         0,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < localResults.size(); offset += chunkSize) {
+            const size_t count = std::min(chunkSize, localResults.size() - offset);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + offset,
+                     static_cast<int>(count),
+                     MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     0,
+                     0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+// even when the benchmark is run with a very large option count.
